Privacy & security
Recent activity
Keep track of recent files
Start and File Explorer show the files you opened recently. Only you see this list; it stays on this computer.
On
[aria-label] Keep track of recent files
Clear recent files history
Empty the list of recently opened files. The files themselves stay.
Clear
How Win11 Shell keeps you safe
Deleted files go to the Recycle Bin
Files are never erased straight away. You can restore them until you empty the Recycle Bin.
Asking first
Before something important happens, like shutting down or cleaning up, Win11 Shell asks if you’re sure.
Administrator rights
Some tasks need your password. Win11 Shell always explains why first, then Ubuntu asks for your password.
Everything stays on this computer
Win11 Shell doesn’t send any data about you or your files to the internet.

More options
New Ubuntu versions
An upgrade every six months; an interim version gets 9 months of updates.
Every interim version has a new GNOME, and Win11 Shell has to be ready for it first.
Ubuntu 26.10 is out; Ubuntu doesn’t offer the upgrade yet.
[aria-label] New Ubuntu versions
Every new version
Ubuntu asks for your password to run ‘/bin/sh’ as administrator. That’s Win11 Shell, changing only the Prompt= line.

Check for updates
Win11 Shell needs to restartParts of the desktop were updated (libsoup). Restarting takes a few seconds; Win11 Shell windows close briefly.
Restart Win11 Shell
More options
New Ubuntu versions
An upgrade every six months; an interim version gets 9 months of updates.
Every interim version has a new GNOME, and Win11 Shell has to be ready for it first.
Ubuntu 26.10 is out; Ubuntu doesn’t offer the upgrade yet.
[aria-label] New Ubuntu versions
Every new version
Ubuntu asks for your password to run ‘/bin/sh’ as administrator. That’s Win11 Shell, changing only the Prompt= line.
Update history

Restart notifications, metered connections, Ubuntu Software Updater

# title: Task Manager – Settings
Task Manager

[aria-label] Search

[data-tooltip] Maximise
[aria-label] Close
[data-tooltip] Close
[aria-label] Open navigation
[aria-label] Task Manager
Summary
Processes
Performance
Storage
Startup apps
Services
Details
Settings
Settings
[aria-label] Settings
General
Real-time update speed
How often Task Manager refreshes its data. Slower uses less power.
[aria-label] Real-time update speed
Normal (every second)
Default start page
The page you see when you open Task Manager.
[aria-label] Default start page
Summary
Always on top
Keeps Task Manager above other windows and on every desktop.
Off
Graphs
Colour-keyed graphs
Processor blue, memory purple, disk green, network orange and graphics teal. Off = everything in your accent colour.
On
Graph length
How much history the graphs show.
[aria-label] Graph length
60 seconds
Safety
Ask before ending a task
You’ll get an explanation first, so you don’t lose any work.
On
Ask before turning on efficiency mode
Explains what efficiency mode does.
On
About Task Manager
Task Manager for Win11 Shell
Version 0.1.0. All data comes from your own PC; nothing is sent anywhere. Test mode: actions aren’t really carried out.

[aria-label] Search tips
Search tips
[aria-label] Close
[data-tooltip] Close
Just type part of a name. These characters and words make your search more precise. Click an example to try it.
The basics
photo
Finds everything with “photo” in the name, including “Holiday photos”.
holiday 2024
Space: all words must match.
"my document"
Quotes: exactly these words, in this order.
Combining
jpg|png
Bar: one or the other.
report !draft
Exclamation mark: not this word.
<photo|video> 2024
Angle brackets group part of your search.
Wildcards
*.pdf
Asterisk: any characters. These are all PDF files.
IMG_00??.jpg
Question mark: exactly one character.
Filters
ext:mp3;flac
Files with these extension(s).
size:>1gb
Larger than 1 GB. Also: size:<10mb or size:10mb..100mb.
dm:today
Changed today. Also: yesterday, thisweek, thismonth, thisyear.
dm:2026-10-01..2026-10-25
Changed between two dates.
folder:holiday
Folders only (with “holiday” in the name).
file: notes
Files only.
pic:
Pictures only. Also: audio:, video:, doc:, zip:, exe:.
empty:
Empty folders.
Places
parent:~/Downloads
Everything directly in this folder.
in:~/Documents invoice
Everything in this folder and the folders inside it.
path:work quote
Search folder names too: files in a folder “work”.
Advanced
case:IMG
Match upper and lower case.
ww:folder
Only the whole word “folder”, not “folders”.
regex:^IMG_\d+
Regular expression for exact patterns.
Keyboard shortcuts
Enter
Open
Ctrl
+
Enter
Open containing folder
Ctrl
+
C
Copy path
Ctrl
+
F
Go to the search box
Alt
+
Enter
Properties

Initialise Schijf 3
Choose a partition style for this disk.
GPT (GUID Partition Table) — recommended
For modern computers and disks larger than 2 TB.
MBR (Master Boot Record)
Only for very old computers and devices.
All volumes and data on this disk will be erased. Ubuntu recognises nothing on this disk, but it may still hold data Ubuntu can’t read (for example from VeraCrypt, Storage Spaces or a RAID system). That data will be erased too.

More options
New Ubuntu versions
An upgrade every six months; an interim version gets 9 months of updates.
Every interim version has a new GNOME, and Win11 Shell has to be ready for it first.
Ubuntu 26.10 is out; Ubuntu doesn’t offer the upgrade yet.
[aria-label] New Ubuntu versions
Every new version
Ubuntu asks for your password to run ‘/bin/sh’ as administrator. That’s Win11 Shell, changing only the Prompt= line.

More options
New Ubuntu versions
An upgrade every two years; each version gets 5 years of updates.
You’re on an interim version, so Ubuntu still offers every new version until the next LTS.
[aria-label] New Ubuntu versions
LTS versions only
Ubuntu asks for your password to run ‘/bin/sh’ as administrator. That’s Win11 Shell, changing only the Prompt= line.

Ubuntu 26.10 is available
The latest version of Ubuntu, with new features. Gets 9 months of updates, until July 2027.
✓ Win11 Shell works with Ubuntu 26.10.
See what’s in this update
Download and install
Before you upgrade
ffmpeg is missing from Ubuntu 26.10; video thumbnails won’t work then.
Ubuntu turns off 2 extra software sources during the upgrade, such as dl.google.com.
More options

Test mode
On (nothing is actually changed)
Upgrade to Ubuntu 26.10?
Wait for a compatible Win11 version. As soon as Win11 Shell is ready for this version, you’ll see the upgrade here.
Ubuntu does the upgrade itself, in a terminal window. It usually takes 30 to 90 minutes and Ubuntu asks for your password. Save your work, plug a laptop into its charger, and don’t close the window until Ubuntu asks to restart.
Upgrade anyway
Cancel

Upgrade to Ubuntu 26.10?
Ubuntu does the upgrade itself, in a terminal window. It usually takes 30 to 90 minutes and Ubuntu asks for your password. Save your work, plug a laptop into its charger, and don’t close the window until Ubuntu asks to restart.
Your laptop is running on battery. Plug it in before you start.
You’re on a metered connection. An upgrade often downloads more than 2 GB.

Check for updates
Ubuntu 26.10 is on its way
Wait for a compatible Win11 version. Win11 Shell doesn’t work with GNOME 51 yet, which comes with Ubuntu 26.10.
Ubuntu won’t offer this upgrade itself until Win11 Shell is ready for it.
Undo
More options
New Ubuntu versions
An upgrade every six months; an interim version gets 9 months of updates.
Every interim version has a new GNOME, and Win11 Shell has to be ready for it first.
[aria-label] New Ubuntu versions
Every new version
Ubuntu asks for your password to run ‘/bin/sh’ as administrator. That’s Win11 Shell, changing only the Prompt= line.
Update history
Advanced options
Restart notifications, metered connections, Ubuntu Software Updater
Settings backup
Save your Win11 Shell settings in a file, or restore them
Export settings
Save all your settings in a file (for example on a USB stick)
Export
Import settings
Restore settings from a file you exported earlier
Choose file
Reset everything
Reset all Win11 Shell settings to their defaults. Your files are kept.
